(from the previous step)  \\n- **Output format:** Model weights (H5 format)  \\n- **Output filename:** `sales_model.h5`  \\n\\n4. **Predict Future Sales:** Use the trained model to predict sales for the next 10 time intervals.  \\n- **Input filename:** `sales_model.h5` (from the previous step)  \\n- **Output format:** CSV with predictions  \\n- **Output filename:** `predicted_sales.csv`  \\n\\nPlease provide the complete Python code implementation, and ensure that the code structure is clear and well-commented for understanding and evaluation."\\n\\n# Plan Status\\n\\n## Finished Tasks\\n### code\\n```python\\nimport pandas as pd\\nimport numpy as np\\nfile_path = \\\'../train_data.csv\\\'\\nwith open(file_path, \\\'r\\\') as file:\\n    for _ in range(5):\\n        print(file.readline())\\ndata = pd.read_csv(file_path, sep=\\\',\\\')\\nprint("Column names:", data.columns)\\ndata_cleaned = data.copy()\\nmissing_values = data_cleaned.isnull().sum()\\nprint("Missing values in each column:\\\\n", missing_values)\\nif \\\'Sales Time\\\' in data_cleaned.columns:\\n    data_cleaned.dropna(subset=[\\\'Sales Time\\\'], inplace=True)\\nelse:\\n    print("\\\'Sales Time\\\' column not found.")\\ndata_cleaned.fillna({\\n    \\\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\\\': data_cleaned[\\\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\\\'].median(),\\n    \\\'Total Amount (CNY)\\\': data_cleaned[\\\'Total Amount (CNY)\\\'].median(),\\n    \\\'Refund Amount (CNY)\\\': 0\\n}, inplace=True)\\ndata_cleaned.drop_duplicates(inplace=True)\\nif \\\'Sales Time\\\' in data_cleaned.columns:\\n    data_cleaned[\\\'Sales Time\\\'] = pd.to_datetime(data_cleaned[\\\'Sales Time\\\'], errors=\\\'coerce\\\')\\ndef handle_outliers(df, column):\\n    Q1 = df[column].quantile(0.25)\\n    Q3 = df[column].quantile(0.75)\\n    IQR = Q3 - Q1\\n    lower_bound = Q1 - 1.5 * IQR\\n    upper_bound = Q3 + 1.5 * IQR\\n    df[column] = np.where(df[column] < lower_bound, lower_bound, df[column])\\n    df[column] = np.where(df[column] > upper_bound, upper_bound, df[column])\\nfor column in [\\\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\\\', \\\'Total Amount (CNY)\\\', \\\'Refund Amount (CNY)\\\']:\\n    handle_outliers(data_cleaned, column)\\noutput_file_path = \\\'./processed_data.csv\\\'\\ndata_cleaned.to_csv(output_file_path, index=False)\\nprint(f"Data preprocessing complete. Processed data saved to {output_file_path}.")\\n\\nimport pandas as pd\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import MinMaxScaler\\nfrom tensorflow.keras.models import Sequential\\nfrom tensorflow.keras.layers import Dense, LSTM\\nfrom tensorflow.keras.callbacks import EarlyStopping\\ndata = pd.read_csv(\\\'./processed_data.csv\\\')\\ndata[\\\'Sales Time\\\'] = pd.to_datetime(data[\\\'Sales Time\\\'])\\ndata.set_index(\\\'Sales Time\\\', inplace=True)\\nfeatures = [\\\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\\\', \\\'Total Amount (CNY)\\\', \\\'Refund Amount (CNY)\\\']\\ntarget = \\\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\\\'\\nscaler = MinMaxScaler()\\ndata[features] = scaler.fit_transform(data[features])\\ndef create_sequences(data, seq_length):\\n    X, y = [], []\\n    for i in range(len(data) - seq_length):\\n        X.append(data[i:i+seq_length])\\n        y.append(data[i+seq_length])\\n    return np.array(X), np.array(y)\\nseq_length = 10\\nX, y = create_sequences(data[features].values, seq_length)\\ntrain_size = int(len(X) * 0.8)\\nX_train, X_test = X[:train_size], X[train_size:]\\ny_train, y_test = y[:train_size], y[train_size:]\\nmodel = Sequential([\\n    LSTM(50, activation=\\\'relu\\\', input_shape=(seq_length, len(features))),\\n    Dense(1)\\n])\\nmodel.compile(optimizer=\\\'adam\\\', loss=\\\'mse\\\')\\nearly_stopping = EarlyStopping(monitor=\\\'val_loss\\\', patience=5, restore_best_weights=True)\\nmodel.fit(X_train, y_train, validation_data=(X_test, y_test), epochs=100, callbacks=[early_stopping])\\nmodel.save(\\\'./sales_model.h5\\\')\\nprint("Model training complete. Model weights saved to \\\'./sales_model.h5\\\'.")\\n```\\n\\n### execution result\\n\\ufeffSales Time,Purchase Quantity (pcs),Total Amount (CNY),Payment Method,Refund Amount (CNY),Product Details\\n\\n2018-04-01 01:00,2,5,支付宝,0,可口可乐\\n\\n2018-04-01 01:00,4,12,支付宝,0,可口可乐\\n\\n2018-04-01 01:00,3,7.5,微信,0,雪碧\\n\\n2018-04-01 03:00,9,22.5,微信,0,可口可乐\\n\\nColumn names: Index([\\\'Sales Time\\\', \\\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\\\', \\\'Total Amount (CNY)\\\',\\n       \\\'Payment Method\\\', \\\'Refund Amount (CNY)\\\', \\\'Product Details\\\'],\\n      dtype=\\\'object\\\')\\nMissing values in each column:\\n Sales Time                 0\\nPurchase Quantity (pcs)    0\\nTotal Amount (CNY)         0\\nPayment Method             0\\nRefund Amount (CNY)        0\\nProduct Details            0\\ndtype: int64\\nData preprocessing complete. found.")\ndata_cleaned.fillna({\n    \'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\': data_cleaned[\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\'].median(),\n    \'Total Amount (CNY)\': data_cleaned[\'Total Amount (CNY)\'].median(),\n    \'Refund Amount (CNY)\': 0\n}, inplace=True)\ndata_cleaned.drop_duplicates(inplace=True)\nif \'Sales Time\' in data_cleaned.columns:\n    data_cleaned[\'Sales Time\'] = pd.to_datetime(data_cleaned[\'Sales Time\'], errors=\'coerce\')\ndef handle_outliers(df, column):\n    Q1 = df[column].quantile(0.25)\n    Q3 = df[column].quantile(0.75)\n    IQR = Q3 - Q1\n    lower_bound = Q1 - 1.5 * IQR\n    upper_bound = Q3 + 1.5 * IQR\n    df[column] = np.where(df[column] < lower_bound, lower_bound, df[column])\n    df[column] = np.where(df[column] > upper_bound, upper_bound, df[column])\nfor column in [\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\', \'Total Amount (CNY)\', \'Refund Amount (CNY)\']:\n    handle_outliers(data_cleaned, column)\noutput_file_path = \'./processed_data.csv\'\ndata_cleaned.to_csv(output_file_path, index=False)\nprint(f"Data preprocessing complete. Processed data saved to {output_file_path}.")\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import MinMaxScaler\nfrom tensorflow.keras.models import Sequential\nfrom tensorflow.keras.layers import Dense, LSTM\nfrom tensorflow.keras.callbacks import EarlyStopping\ndata = pd.read_csv(\'./processed_data.csv\')\ndata[\'Sales Time\'] = pd.to_datetime(data[\'Sales Time\'])\ndata.set_index(\'Sales Time\', inplace=True)\nfeatures = [\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\', \'Total Amount (CNY)\', \'Refund Amount (CNY)\']\ntarget = \'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\'\nscaler = MinMaxScaler()\ndata[features] = scaler.fit_transform(data[features])\ndef create_sequences(data, seq_length):\n    X, y = [], []\n    for i in range(len(data) - seq_length):\n        X.append(data[i:i+seq_length])\n        y.append(data[i+seq_length])\n    return np.array(X), np.array(y)\nseq_length = 10\nX, y = create_sequences(data[features].values, seq_length)\ntrain_size = int(len(X) * 0.8)\nX_train, X_test = X[:train_size], X[train_size:]\ny_train, y_test = y[:train_size], y[train_size:]\nmodel = Sequential([\n    LSTM(50, activation=\'relu\', input_shape=(seq_length, len(features))),\n    Dense(1)\n])\nmodel.compile(optimizer=\'adam\', loss=\'mse\')\nearly_stopping = EarlyStopping(monitor=\'val_loss\', patience=5, restore_best_weights=True)\nmodel.fit(X_train, y_train, validation_data=(X_test, y_test), epochs=100, callbacks=[early_stopping])\nmodel.save(\'./sales_model.h5\')\nprint("Model training complete. Model weights saved to \'./sales_model.h5\'.")\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom tensorflow.keras.models import load_model\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import MinMaxScaler\ndata = pd.read_csv(\'./processed_data.csv\')\ndata[\'Sales Time\'] = pd.to_datetime(data[\'Sales Time\'])\ndata.set_index(\'Sales Time\', inplace=True)\nfeatures = [\'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\', \'Total Amount (CNY)\', \'Refund Amount (CNY)\']\ntarget = \'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\'\nscaler = MinMaxScaler()\ndata[features] = scaler.fit_transform(data[features])\nmodel = load_model(\'./sales_model.h5\', compile=False)\nseq_length = 10\nX, _ = create_sequences(data[features].values, seq_length)\npredictions = model.predict(X[-1].reshape(1, seq_length, len(features)))\npredicted_sales = scaler.inverse_transform(np.concatenate((predictions, np.zeros((predictions.shape[0], len(features) - 1))), axis=1))[:, 0]\npredicted_sales_df = pd.DataFrame(predicted_sales, columns=[\'Predicted Purchase Quantity (pcs)\'])\npredicted_sales_df.to_csv(\'./predicted_sales.csv\', index=False)\nprint("Prediction complete. Predicted sales saved to \'./predicted_sales.csv\'.")\n```\n\n### execution result\n\ufeffSales Time,Purchase Quantity (pcs),Total Amount (CNY),Payment Method,Refund Amount (CNY),Product Details\n\n2018-04-01 01:00,2,5,支付宝,0,可口可乐\n\n2018-04-01 01:00,4,12,支付宝,0,可口可乐\n\n2018-04-01 01:00,3,7.5,微信,0,雪碧\n\n2018-04-01 03:00,9,22.5,微信,0,可口可乐\n\nColumn names: Index([\'Sales Time\', \'Purchase Quantity (pcs)\', \'Total Amount (CNY)\',\n       \'Payment Method\', \'Refund Amount (CNY)\', \'Product Details\'],\n      dtype=\'object\')\nMissing values in each column:\n Sales Time                 0\nPurchase Quantity (pcs)    0\nTotal Amount (CNY)         0\nPayment Method             0\nRefund Amount (CNY)        0\nProduct Details            0\ndtype: int64\nData preprocessing complete.
